By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [180] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计结果排序期望（DOOR）概率，以进行临床试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评估。该框架将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函，通过序贯风险集危险函数估计条件有序分布，并推导了DOOR概率的高效影响函数（EIF）。模拟研究显示，TMLE-SL和CVTMLE-SL分别在点估计和推断性能上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机试验和观察性研究中，获益-风险评估需要同时权衡疗效与安全性，DOOR概率是常用的评价指标。然而，现有的DOOR概率估计方法缺乏统一的协变量校正框架，且在存在混杂、治疗效应异质性等复杂情况下，尚缺乏系统的估计和推断方法比较。

Method: 将DOOR概率表示为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函；通过序贯风险集危险函数估计条件有序分布；推导DOOR概率的高效影响函数（EIF）；点估计比较了G-computation、归一化逆概率加权（IPW）、增强IPW（AIPW）和目标最大似然估计（TMLE），其中干扰函数分别用广义线性模型或Super Learner（SL）估计；EIF推断评估了AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL（含/不含交叉拟合），跨不同的重叠度、治疗效应异质性和治疗分配设置；最后用抗菌耐药领导集团的多重耐药菌网络数据进行了实例分析。

Result: TMLE-SL在点估计中表现最强且最一致，AIPW-SL位居第二；CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖率方面整体表现最优。

Conclusion: 建议在实践中优先考虑使用TMLE-SL进行点估计、CVTMLE-SL进行推断，该框架为临床获益-风险评估提供了统一且稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [181] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将混合效应模型推广到深度学习的新型神经网络架构（Deep Generalised Mixed Model, DGMM），用于分析经验采样法（ESM）收集的纵向数据。该模型通过变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法进行估计，能够处理任意分布的纵向结果、适应高维设置，并在数据随机缺失（MAR）时提供有效推断。在GrowIt!青少年情绪研究中的应用和模拟实验显示该模型有潜力，但因模型不稳定而表现欠佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验采样法（ESM）数据分析面临两大挑战：标准统计方法难以扩展到高维设置，而机器学习方法因数据缺失（特别是参与者因负面情绪而退出导致的随机缺失）会产生有偏结果。现有方法无法同时解决这两个问题，需要一种既能灵活建模、又能在缺失数据下提供有效推断的新方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种将混合效应模型推广到深度学习的神经网络架构（Deep Generalised Mixed Model）。该模型通过固定效应和随机效应实现对数据均值和相关结构的半参数灵活建模；估计过程采用变分自编码器（VAE）的适配版本以及贝叶斯数据增强算法，能够处理服从一般分布的纵向结果，适应高维设定并在随机缺失（MAR）数据下提供有效推断。

Result: 将该模型应用于GrowIt!研究数据（COVID-19疫情期间青少年每日情绪）以及多项模拟实验。结果表明DGMM具有应用潜力，但由于模型不稳定，整体表现欠佳。

Conclusion: DGMM为ESM数据提供了一种融合深度学习与混合效应模型优势的新思路，能同时应对高维性和随机缺失问题，但目前模型稳定性仍有待改进，需要进一步优化才能在实际应用中达到理想性能。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [182] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概率回归树（PRTrees）框架，使其能够直接在树构建过程中处理缺失预测变量，无需事先填补。提出了三种利用不同可用信息的策略，并在多组真实数据集上验证了其性能优于传统CART回归树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归树在处理缺失预测变量时需要事先填补，这引入了额外偏差且效率低下。而PRTrees虽已证明是经典回归树的平滑且一致的有效替代方案，但尚未扩展以处理缺失值。本文旨在将PRTree框架扩展到缺失数据场景，消除对先验填补的依赖。

Method: 提出三种处理缺失值的策略：(1) 均匀概率方法（uniform-probability）；(2) 部分观测方法（partial-observation）；(3) 降维平滑方法（dimension-reduced smoothing）。这些修改保留了原始PRTree的关键概率性质，包括概率守恒和边际相容性，适用于任意缺失模式。

Result: 在多个不同缺失程度的真实数据集上的实验表明，缺失观测的处理方式对概率树构建的预测效果有显著影响。其中fill策略是主导建模组件，影响力甚至超过平滑分布或代理选择准则。在缺失比例较高的数据集中，所提方法多项指标优于CART，同时保持了树模型的解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 直接在树构建中处理缺失值的PRTree扩展方法在缺失数据场景下优于传统回归树，且fill策略的选择是影响预测性能的关键因素，为缺失数据处理与树模型结合提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部化保形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限样本理论保证，在Hölder光滑性等假设下，对任意固定分数证明了局部条件覆盖率缺口和相对最优预言机的长度误差的高概率一致界，并将误差分解为局部化偏置与校准项之和，揭示了带宽的偏置-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测虽能提供有限样本、无分布假设的边缘覆盖率保证，但边缘有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特定校准误差，而精确的无分布条件覆盖率在有限样本下不可实现。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来缓解这一问题，但现有理论缺乏对实际局部化集合同时控制条件有效性和预言效率的有限样本保证。

Method: 在分数条件CDF满足Hölder正则性以及标准密度和核假设下，证明了高概率界，且在实现的局部化邻域上一致成立，同时控制条件覆盖率缺口和相对预言机的长度误差。误差界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏置项和随校准集规模增大而减小的校准项。此外还分析了数据分裂学习的分数，表明当分数趋近于枢轴分数（如保形分位数回归）时，局部保证可分解为固定分数校准和分数估计误差两部分。

Result: 建立了RLCP的有限样本高概率一致界，统一地控制了条件有效性和预言效率；证明了误差来源的分解结构，阐明带宽偏置-方差权衡以及RLCP何时逼近预言机；对于学习分数的情况，证明改进的分数学习可增强局部化保证。

Conclusion: RLCP在有限样本下能够在条件校准性和预言效率之间实现可量化的平衡，理论界清晰揭示了各误差来源的贡献，为实践中带宽选择和分数学习策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [184] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化分类中，对带有标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型，早停（early stopping）可以使梯度下降（GD）实现极小极大最优的0-1风险，优于最大间隔插值分类器的隐式偏置，并建立了新的校准结果将logistic风险转换为0-1风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过参数化分类中，数据可以线性可分即使真实分布不可分。GD在logistic损失下范数发散但方向收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏置在统计上可能是次优的。因此需要探索早停等替代策略来克服这种次优性。

Method: 通过分析高斯混合模型（带标签翻转噪声）中早停的GD迭代：1）推导早停迭代的尖锐上界；2）匹配任意分类器的统计下界；3）提出新的校准结果，将excess logistic风险转换为excess 0-1风险，处理模型误设定并去除标准界中的平方根率；4）建立线性插值器的下界。

Result: 早停在合适的oracle时间停止时，对具有快速连续衰减的协方差谱（包括多项式与指数衰减）的GD实现了极小极大最优的excess 0-1风险。线性插值器要达到同等excess风险可能需要指数更多的样本。

Conclusion: 早停是过参数化分类中克服最大间隔插值器统计次优性的有效工具，能实现极小极大最优，且实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [185] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于强化学习的框架，用于在持久性图（PD）空间上进行随机动力学建模，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义受控马尔可夫过程，并证明其不可约、非周期且几何遍历，从而保证PD空间上唯一平稳分布的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PD统计分析方法大多将持久性图视为静态对象，缺乏对PD空间上进行概率建模和随机演化的系统框架，难以支持自适应的拓扑简化、压缩及科学目标驱动的动力学过程。

Method: 引入强化学习框架，将PD演化构建为经由拓扑感知的局部编辑操作的受控马尔可夫过程，在变基数的有限PD空间上定义动力学；建立马尔可夫链不可约性、非周期性与几何遍历性的条件；设计综合奖励函数，涵盖分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计量以及结构保持压缩。

Result: 在合成数据和神经影像PD上的实验表明，所提框架能够在降低图复杂度的同时保持主导拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为自适应的拓扑简化和PD空间上的概率建模提供了统一且理论上可保证的方法，填补了PD作为动态对象进行随机演化和受控优化的研究空白。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [186] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了单调对抗者（monotone adversary）在干净样本中插入额外正确标注样本后，学习者的最小最大期望误差。结果表明：VC维d=1时误差为Θ(1/n)，d≥2时误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，额外的对数因子是本质的而非特定算法的伪影。同样的结果对Littlestone维也成立，表明基于在线学习的O(d_L/n)批量学习率无法达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人提出的单调对抗模型打破了样本可交换性，观察到经验风险最小化达到O((d/n)log(n/d))误差，而已知的最优学习者都会被推到Θ(d/n)率之外。关键悬而未决的问题是：这个额外的对数因子究竟是特定算法的缺陷，还是缺乏可交换性导致的固有代价？回答这一问题对于理解PAC学习中样本交换性的作用至关重要。

Method: 作者证明了该额外对数代价在VC维≥2时是本质的。通过单一构造建立所有下界：构造一个显式类别和先验，使得两个仅在非零概率质量点处不同的目标假设产生相同的样本。上界方面，VC维为1时采用简单的不当（improper）学习者，其分析改编了一包含图（one-inclusion graph）背后的留一法（leave-one-out）论证。

Result: 在最坏类别和最坏已知有限插入预算下，最小最大期望误差为：d=1时Θ(1/n)，d≥2时Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。将VC维替换为Littlestone维时获得相同速率，从而证明在线到批量的O(d_L/n)速率同样不可实现。所有下界都由一个统一的显式构造导出。

Conclusion: 添加正确标注的样本反而会使学习变难一个对数因子，即使对于具有有限在线错误界（mistake bound）的类别也是如此。这一结果揭示了样本可交换性在学习中的作用：缺失它会导致即使在标签全部正确的情况下，也存在本质的学习复杂度提升。维度一与维度二以上的速率鸿沟进一步表明该现象与类别的结构复杂度密切相关。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [187] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将VARMA模型估计的计算成本从依赖序列长度T中解放出来的新框架，通过偏自相关重参数化保证平稳性和可逆性，利用Parseval恒等式使损失仅依赖于固定大小的充分统计量，实现每次优化迭代与序列长度无关。该框架产生两个点估计器（正则化最小二乘和协方差边缘化最大后验估计），在固定维度下以近似参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，并扩展到季节性、外生变量和滚动窗口场景，在d=10到d=40维度下接近Oracle预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型长期以来被认为在实际应用中不可行，主要原因有三：似然函数非凸、参数化仅在同构意义下可识别、每次评估都需要遍历整个序列。然而VARMA的移动平均项可以用少量参数捕捉纯自回归需要大量滞后才能匹配的动态。作者的目标是移除这一计算障碍，使VARMA估计的计算成本独立于序列长度。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 偏自相关重参数化，确保平稳性和可逆性（invertibility）在构造上得到保证；(2) 对重参数化系数施加高斯先验，对角线和非对角线条目使用不同尺度；(3) 损失函数仅通过固定大小的充分统计量依赖数据，利用Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式以截断长度的近线性成本求值。由此得到两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计器。

Result: 理论上，两个估计器在固定维度下以近似参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，证明截断不会引入渐近偏差。该框架以相同的主导成本扩展到季节性动态、外部回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。实证上，从d=10到d=40维度下估计器接近Oracle预测误差（经典条件MLE在此范围内产生不可逆拟合导致预测发散），并在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量式ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架使得基于似然的VARMA估计在每次迭代成本独立于序列长度的前提下，可以应用于从业者迄今为止只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模，填补了VARMA模型在大维度实际应用上的主要计算障碍。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轮廓积分方法（CIM），用于在指定时间区间内高效计算参数化线性控制系统的输入输出量，并达到用户指定的精度。该方法通过沿修改的积分轮廓应用求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换，并与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）相结合，采用贪心算法构建投影空间，且自然满足Hermite插值条件，从而大幅降低跨参数域的输入输出关系计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化线性控制系统的输入输出计算在指定时间区间内通常成本高昂，现有方法难以同时兼顾计算效率和精度保证。需要一种能够高效处理参数化系统、支持多种输入函数、并适用于低维子空间捕获初始条件的方法。

Method: 1) 通过修改的积分轮廓上的求积规则近似逆拉普拉斯变换；2) 与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）结合；3) 推导误差估计并基于贪心算法构建投影空间；4) 证明该投影框架自然满足Hermite插值条件。

Result: 在基准的非参数化和参数化控制系统上验证了方法的精度和效率，并与最先进的基于投影的MOR方法进行了对比，展示了显著的性能优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的CIM与投影MOR的组合方法能够在大幅降低成本的同时，保证用户指定精度，适用于广泛的输入函数和参数域，是一种高效且可靠的参数化控制系统求解方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [189] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文求解了一个异质多搜索成本博弈的精确解：Hider将两个相同球分布在三个成本满足a≥b≥c>0的盒子中，Searcher自适应开盒直到找到两个球，每次开盒产生对应成本且最多找回一个球。博弈值由三个显式有理函数的最大值给出，对应三种最优Hider策略和若干精确的Searcher混合策略，并通过Bernstein基证书验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决搜索博弈领域中一个基础但此前未获精确解的异质多搜索成本博弈问题。虽然此前的同质或单目标搜索博弈已有成熟结果，但引入了异质成本和多目标（两个球）的耦合后，博弈结构显著复杂化，需要一个完整且精确的解析刻画。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 将所有非浪费的确定性Searcher策略化简为72个基本决策树，进而归并为42种不同的开盒次数分布；(2) 通过解析论证解决第一个参数区域；(3) 构造精确的Searcher混合策略（第二个区域用两种混合，第三个区域用四种混合）来证明对策值；(4) 利用精确的有理Bernstein基证书来验证参数化混合策略在整个成本区域上的可行性；(5) 通过独立实现复现策略集、零和线性规划值及Bernstein验证器检查的全部154个二进节点。

Result: 博弈值被精确确定为三个显式有理函数的最大值。最优Hider策略为乘积形式分布，分别在三个、五个或全部六个放置位置上施加限制。所有非浪费的确定性搜索策略等价于72个基本决策树（诱导42个不同的开盒次数分布）。三种参数区域分别由解析论证、两种精确混合策略和四种精确混合策略解决。

Conclusion: 该论文为异质多搜索成本博弈提供了完整的精确解，不仅给出了博弈值的闭式表达式和最优策略构造，还通过Bernstein基证书严格验证了参数化混合策略的全局可行性，并辅以多路独立实现交叉验证，确保结果的可靠性。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [190] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对野火期间弱势群体（如医院患者和长期护理居民）的疏散支援问题，提出了一种两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和带严格时间窗的车辆路径规划。为克服NP难复杂性，作者提出基于逻辑型Benders分解的创新求解方法，包含组合型Benders割和逻辑型不等式。基于科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演习的真实数据实验表明，该方法相比替代策略在避难所选址、车辆利用率和整体疏散效率上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火期间弱势群体的疏散支援问题至关重要但研究不足，现有方法难以同时处理设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径的联合优化，且问题具有NP难复杂性，需要高效且高质量的求解方法。

Method: 构建两阶段随机优化模型优化设施选址、车队规模与带严格时间窗的车辆路径；提出基于逻辑型Benders分解（Logic-Based Benders Decomposition）的求解方法，引入组合型Benders割（Combinatorial Benders Cuts）和逻辑型不等式以提升求解效率。

Result: 基于科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区真实野火演习数据的大量数值实验表明，所提方法能够生成高质量解，在避难所布置、车辆利用率及整体疏散效率方面相较替代策略均有显著改善。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段随机优化模型与逻辑型Benders分解方法为野火场景下弱势群体疏散支援提供了高效可行的决策工具，能够显著提升疏散运营的效率和资源利用率。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [191] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价（其被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续、近端有界函数之和。在非光滑、局部化下降引理的结构假设下，利用逐步细化的样本均值逼近和Armijo型线搜索，在无需正则项(弱)凸性及无需随机oracle方差一致有界的宽松条件下，建立了函数值几乎必然收敛和轨迹聚点驻点性的保证，并借助KL性质升级为整条轨迹收敛，对指数型KL函数和多项式增长的样本量给出了显式多项式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法在处理"期望代价（被积函数非光滑非凸）+非凸正则项"的组合问题时存在局限：要么要求正则项的(弱)凸性，要么要求随机oracle方差有界，且样本量增长需满足特定速率。本文旨在突破这些限制，为更广的问题类别建立收敛保证。

Method: 提出近端随机次梯度方法：每次迭代用逐步细化的样本均值替换期望代价；步长通过Armijo型线搜索选取，并强制满足去除随机误差后的充分下降性质；采用非下降且有界的样本量序列；借助KL性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛。

Result: 在样本量序列仅需非降无界（无需预设增长率）的宽松条件下，建立函数值几乎必然收敛及轨迹每个聚点驻点性；在KL性质下获得整条轨迹收敛到单一驻点；对指数型KL去奇异函数及多项式增长的样本量，导出函数值和迭代点的显式多项式收敛速率（含对数因子）。即使在新设定（光滑情形）下，这些收敛保证也是新的。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法支持更一般的问题形式：既不要求正则项(弱)凸，也不要求随机oracle方差一致有界，同时给出了从几乎必然收敛到整条轨迹收敛再到明确收敛速率的完整收敛分析体系。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [192] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带两阶段自适应动量的惯性块邻近线性化方法（IBPL^+-TP），用于求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题。该方法通过两阶段自适应动量策略有效更新外推参数，允许使用两个独立且不受其他参数约束的外推点来加速收敛，同时保证了目标函数的单调收敛性、序列全局收敛到临界点并建立了收敛率。在稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解两个机器学习问题上，该方法优于多种现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛存在于地震前异常分析、机器学习等实际应用中，而现有的求解方法在加速收敛、外推参数选取灵活性以及收敛性理论保证方面存在不足，需要一种既能有效加速收敛又具有严格理论保障的新方法。

Method: 提出惯性块邻近线性化方法（IBPL^+-TP），其核心创新包括：(1)采用两阶段自适应动量策略动态更新外推参数；(2)同时使用两个不同的外推点以加速收敛；(3)两个外推点的外推参数相互独立且不受其他参数约束。在此基础上，证明了目标函数的单调收敛性、序列的全局收敛性到临界点，并建立了收敛率。

Result: 将该方法应用于两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题（带ℓ0约束的稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解），数值实验结果表明该方法优于多个最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的IBPL^+-TP方法在保持快速收敛优势的同时具备严格的收敛性理论保证，在多块非凸非光滑优化问题中展现出优越的实际性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [193] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的迭代收敛框架，适用于广泛的Bregman近端方法（BPMs）和复合目标函数，通过扩展缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质，建立了离散时间（BPM）和连续时间（镜像流）的轨迹收敛理论，无需凸性或孤立性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPMs的迭代收敛问题长期未解，尤其是非凸目标情形。尽管chen2026skl通过SKŁ性质取得进展，但仅适用于Shannon熵核和线性约束问题，缺乏通用性。

Method: 引入核依赖的参数化函数来扩展SKŁ性质，证明其对所有连续半解析函数成立（特别是当核具有闭域时）；基于此框架验证标准BPMs在温和正则条件下满足假设；并利用参数化函数分析连续时间BPM（镜像流）的轨迹收敛。

Result: 扩展的SKŁ性质对所有连续次解析函数成立；标准BPMs在温和正则条件下满足框架假设，建立广泛的迭代收敛结果；镜像流在o-极小平凡可定义目标函数下收敛到驻点，首次在没有凸性或孤立性假设的情况下得到轨迹收敛结果。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了离散和连续时间下BPMs的轨迹收敛理论，显著拓展了此前仅适用于Shannon熵核与线性约束的结果，为更广泛的核与目标函数提供收敛保证。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [194] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重定心域策略的Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），利用离线训练的物理信息神经网络（PINNs）和主成分分析构建密度演化的低维代理模型，在线预测固定窗口内的概率密度演化，实现高效的非线性目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计转化为Forward Kolmogorov方程（FKE）的概率密度传播问题，但在有限计算域上高效近似FKE是将其应用于目标跟踪的关键挑战。传统方法（如EKF、PF）在非线性强约束场景下精度受限或计算成本高。

Method: RD-YYF包含离线与在线两个阶段：离线阶段用PINNs生成FKE解快照，并通过PCA构建密度演化的低维表示，训练一个轻量残差代理模型将初始条件系数和域中心映射到终端解系数；在线阶段在重定心窗口内用预训练代理预测密度演化，随后执行观测更新和状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束目标跟踪实验上，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）和粒子滤波（PF），同时保持高效的逐时间步推理。消融实验表明域重定心改善了高概率区域的密度近似精度并加速了离线PINN收敛。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明RD-YYF在非线性目标跟踪中兼顾精度与效率，展示了其在约束跟踪场景下的应用潜力。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [195] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的Benders型分解算法软件包，用于求解多阶段随机整数规划问题，通过ReLU割确保混合整数状态变量的收敛性，并提出了两种割选择策略（基于对偶问题的归一化和正则化）以及交替割标准来平衡计算成本与割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划在实际中具有广泛应用，但其求解极具挑战性。已有的Benders型分解算法在处理一般混合整数状态变量时缺乏收敛保证，且对偶问题生成的割存在多最优解问题，不同解产生的割对子问题代价函数的逼近质量差异显著，需要有效的策略来优化割选择以加速收敛。

Method: 该软件包实现了具有收敛保证的Benders型分解算法，整合了Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割以及ReLU割。针对对偶问题多最优解问题，提出了两种割选择策略：一是对偶问题的归一化策略，二是正则化策略。此外，还设计了交替割标准，在Benders割有效时使用更廉价的割，仅在必要时才调用更昂贵的紧割。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划问题上进行了基准计算实验，验证了所提出方法的有效性，并揭示了问题结构对算法实际性能的影响规律。

Conclusion: 本文通过开源软件包实现了具有理论收敛保证的分解算法，并结合割选择策略与交替割标准，在计算效率与割质量之间取得了良好平衡，为多阶段随机整数规划提供了一套实用的求解工具。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [196] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集上变分不等式的连续时间投影神经网络，针对一类既非Hessian生成也非状态依赖标量的矩阵度规，证明了投影关于自变量与度规的联合正则性，并在Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度规下建立了局部指数收敛（含显式速率与半径），在紧可行集上通过显式度规变差界实现全局指数收敛。此外，针对二次连续可微、一致正定的度规与强单调线性算子，证明了周期轨道环的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影神经网络的收敛性分析仅覆盖两类度规：由Hessian生成的逆度规（可用Bregman距离消除度规导数项）和状态依赖的标量度规。对于一般的矩阵度规（其逆既非Hessian生成、亦非标量形式），缺乏相应的收敛性理论，因此需要扩展分析框架。

Method: 作者考虑状态依赖的矩阵度规，其逆度规定义投影度量。首先证明投影在自变量与度规两个参数上的联合正则性；在此基础上，利用谱界将欧氏Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为线性界；随后针对Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度规，通过Lyapunov分析建立局部指数收敛的显式速率与半径；在紧可行集上利用度规变差界获得全局指数收敛；最后通过强单调线性算子与非Hessian度规构造周期轨道环并给出解析验证与确定性数值计算。

Result: 获得了投影映射的联合正则性定理；欧氏Lipschitz常量从平方界改进到线性界；建立了局部与全局指数收敛的显式界（速率与半径）；证明了非Hessian矩阵度规下周期轨道环的存在性；确定性数值计算验证了解析公式并量化了收敛证书中的松弛量。

Conclusion: 本文将投影神经网络的收敛性理论从Hessian度规与标量度规推广到一般的状态依赖矩阵度规，建立了联合正则性、显式指数收敛速率与周期轨道现象，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果的紧致性与实际松弛程度。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [197] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆优化方法，旨在一同学习专家决策者的偏好（感知成本）和决策规则，从而从观测到的决策行为中构建可解释的决策模型。通过三个案例（班次分配、生产计划和实际路径规划问题）验证了该方法的通用性，结果表明同时建模感知成本和决策规则能带来更好的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家的决策策略经过多年经验积累，通常难以直接获取，因为隐含的偏好和决策规则很难被明确表达。作者希望通过观测专家的决策行为，学习一个可解释的模型来捕捉其决策过程，这对于培训新手、提升人机系统性能以及实现全自主系统具有重要意义。

Method: 采用逆优化（inverse optimization）框架，联合学习决策者的感知成本（偏好）和决策规则（决定其选择的行为准则）。该方法同时从观测数据中推断这两类参数，从而构建能复现专家决策行为的可解释模型。

Result: 通过三个案例研究（班次分配问题、生产计划问题和实际路径规划问题）验证了方法的普遍适用性。在所有案例中，同时建模感知成本和决策规则相比仅建模单一因素能获得更好的预测效果，证明了该框架的灵活性和有效性。

Conclusion: 同时学习感知成本和决策规则的逆优化框架能够更全面地捕捉和复现专家决策行为，提供了比传统方法更高的灵活性和预测准确性，在复杂工程应用中具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [198] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在希尔伯特空间中求解可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法，通过扩展干摩擦性质为非凸的"类干摩擦"性质并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数，实现了到临界点的子序列收敛或完整序列收敛，并在Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz（KL）性质下保证了完整序列收敛，同时利用Moreau包络的变分性质将算法扩展到弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的惯性梯度算法在处理非凸优化问题时面临收敛性保证不足和参数选择困难的问题。现有的干摩擦性质仅适用于凸函数，缺乏非凸场景下的理论支撑。同时，如何将带有Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法统一到一个通用框架中，并推广到非光滑弱凸优化问题，是亟待解决的挑战。

Method: 首先将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸设置下的"类干摩擦"（dry-like friction）性质；然后采用线搜索技术（line search）在每次迭代中自适应更新参数。通过灵活的参数选择，算法可以退化到多种现有的带Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法。此外，利用Moreau包络的变分性质，将算法扩展到弱凸非光滑优化问题，并将凸KL函数的Moreau包络KL指数结果推广到更广泛的未必凸或连续的一类KL函数。

Result: 在参数的不同选择下，算法实现到目标函数临界点的子序列收敛或到"近似"临界点的完整序列收敛；在效度函数满足KL性质时建立完整序列收敛性。仿真实验验证了算法的效率，并展示了将类干摩擦与外推和线搜索技术结合时的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 该算法提供了一个统一的框架，涵盖了多种现有惯性梯度算法，并通过类干摩擦性质和线搜索技术的结合，在非凸和弱凸非光滑场景下都具备坚实的收敛性保证。其理论推广（包括KL性质下的收敛性和Moreau包络的KL指数扩展）为该领域后续研究奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [199] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过最小化Bregman损失拟合线性回归模型时的优化几何性质。即使生成势函数φ是强凸的，所得的回归目标在参数θ上可能非凸，这与生成势的凸性存在差距。作者将Hessian矩阵表示为加权Gram矩阵，其权重依赖于势函数的导数与当前残差，并基于此给出了局部强凸性、光滑性以及梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件。针对二次-四次势函数，推导了精确的标量凸性条件，刻画了负曲率区间，并给出了正曲率的局部与全局充分条件。数值实验表明标量条件可能在Hessian保持正定时失效，且梯度下降收敛的步长范围随四次项参数增大而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于揭示反向Bregman回归中一个关键的理论缺口：即使生成势函数本身是强凸的，通过该势函数诱导的Bregman损失所定义的回归目标在参数空间中可能呈现非凸性。这一现象意味着生成势的凸性与拟合模型的优化几何之间存在本质性差异，现有理论无法直接解释或保证梯度下降类算法在这些非凸目标上的行为，因此需要从Hessian的结构出发深入理解问题的几何特性。

Method: 本文将回归目标的Hessian矩阵表示为加权Gram矩阵形式，其中权重由势函数的二阶导数（以及更高阶导数）与当前残差共同决定。基于这一表示，推导出局部强凸性和光滑性的简单判定条件，以及梯度下降的条件线性收敛性。针对二次-四次势函数（quadratic-quartic potential），通过代数分析推导出精确的标量凸性条件、负曲率区间以及正曲率的局部和全局充分条件，并通过数值实验验证理论结果。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) Hessian的加权Gram矩阵表示给出了局部强凸性、光滑性及梯度下降条件线性收敛的简洁充分条件；(2) 对二次-四次势导出了精确的标量凸性条件，识别出负曲率区间；(3) 给出了正曲率的局部与全局充分条件；(4) 数值实验发现标量凸性条件可能过于保守——在标量条件不满足时完整Hessian仍可保持正定；(5) 实验表明梯度下降收敛的步长范围随四次参数增大而收缩。

Conclusion: 这些结果系统刻画了残差依赖的曲率如何与生成势函数及设计矩阵相互作用，揭示了反向Bregman回归中目标函数非凸性的来源与边界条件。该工作弥合了生成势凸性与优化几何之间的理论差距，为理解此类非凸回归问题的优化行为提供了理论基础，也为后续设计自适应步长或正则化策略提供了指导。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [200] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DASSP的单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法，用于求解约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）中的离散优化问题。该方法通过子空间分裂重构将Stiefel流形约束与非线性二次约束解耦，每个迭代仅包含谱最小化、投影梯度和阻尼对偶上升三个步骤，是首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT是描述电子激发态和电荷局域态的重要框架，但其离散优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构而面临收敛困难，亟需一种高效可靠的求解算法。

Method: 首先利用内在的旋转不变性并引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂重构，将两组约束解耦；然后基于该重构提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（DASSP），每次迭代包括谱最小化步骤、投影梯度步骤和阻尼对偶上升步骤。

Result: 在合成和真实的CDFT问题上，数值结果表明DASSP达到了高可行性精度，且在保持鲁棒性的同时展现出良好的效率。

Conclusion: DASSP是首个针对CDFT计算具有严格收敛保证的算法，有望为可靠高效的大规模CDFT应用铺平道路。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [201] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究图的度序列优化问题：在给定图中寻找一个子图，使得所有顶点上给定函数在其子图度数处的值之和最大化。作者聚焦二次函数的复杂度，引入了图的临界根（critical roots）概念，并证明当二次根变化时，问题的最优值在临界根定义的区间上是凸分段仿射的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题在组合优化和图论中具有广泛的应用背景（如网络设计、图划分等），但其复杂度尚未被充分理解。特别地，对于二次函数这一重要的函数类，其最优值随参数变化的规律和计算复杂度是一个基础且具有挑战性的问题，需要对问题进行更深入的结构性刻画。

Method: 作者引入"临界根"（critical roots）这一新的图论概念，通过对图的临界根进行分析，刻画了最优值作为二次根的函数在区间上的行为。利用凸分段仿射函数的性质，系统地分析了该优化问题在二次函数情形下的结构和复杂度。

Result: 证明了二次函数情形下度序列优化问题的最优值，随着二次根的变化，在临界根所定义的区间上呈现出凸分段仿射的形式，从而对该问题的结构性质给出了完整刻画。

Conclusion: 通过引入临界根，本文为二次函数情形的度序列优化问题提供了结构性理解，揭示了最优值的凸分段仿射特性，为后续研究该问题的计算复杂度奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [202] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定性的条件下，通过最大化加权覆盖并保持对位置扰动的鲁棒性，实现安全敏感环境中的高效监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际部署中面临位置不确定性，且不同监控区域的重要性（优先级）不同，现有方法未能同时兼顾加权覆盖最大化与位置扰动的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建融合监控约束与RRF鲁棒性公式的数学优化模型，并提出优先级感知鲁棒方向优化（PAROO）算法来确定传感器方向以最大化加权覆盖目标。

Result: 在机场场景的实验中，该框架有效将传感资源导向高优先级区域，较代表性基线方法实现了更高的加权覆盖。

Conclusion: 所提框架在安全敏感环境中具有实际应用价值，能够为空中传感器网络提供兼顾鲁棒性和优先级感知的覆盖优化方案。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [203] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层级防御-攻击者模型（BDAM）提供精确最优解。该方法通过下层级对偶化将主导的攻击者路径项转化为精确的单层级混合整数规划（MILP），可在18个基准配置上证明最优性，并指出此前元启发式结果平均低3.55%，同时揭示了补给规则在平局情形下的不适定问题，并提出平局不变的威胁走廊公式化及MILP-DA修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层拦截模型常用元启发式求解，但在所测试的规模下无法获得精确最优解，因而解的质量无法评估。作者此前用混合元启发式求解BDAM，现需要提供精确最优解作为基准以评估其质量。

Method: (1) 利用弧长是防御者二元决策的线性函数这一特性，通过对下层级进行对偶化，将BDAM中占主导的攻击者最短路径项重构为精确的单层级MILP；(2) 提出威胁走廊公式化以解决补给规则在最短路径平局情形下的不适定问题，该公式化具有平局不变性；(3) 构建MILP-DA，将精确锚点与走廊解码及可行性修复相结合。

Result: 在全部18个三行和五行基准配置上证明了最优性，其中17个在10秒内完成；对15×30网格提供了强而未认证的可行解。此前发表的平均结果比该前沿低3.55%，且差距随规模扩大。平局修正使目标函数变化小于10^-3，但实际补给成本变化高达0.83单位。MILP-DA在18个认证配置中胜出13个，在全部18个更大规模配置上胜出，所有失败均发生在三行网格上。

Conclusion: BDAM可以通过下层对偶化获得精确单层级重构，从而在中等规模基准上认证最优性；此前元启发式结果存在系统性偏低；补给规则在平局下的不适定问题被目标权重抑制而非消除；MILP-DA在大多数配置上优于已发表的元启发式结果。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [204] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不确定随机线性二次控制问题，提出了一种新颖的谱分配方法来为策略迭代（PI）获得初始稳定控制器，并设计了一种不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法，最终通过仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代（PI）是解决最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但其初始化阶段（寻找初始稳定控制器）通常依赖完整的模型信息，这对模型无关的PI构成了重大限制。对于具有状态和控制的乘性噪声的随机系统，稳定性由包含漂移和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式而非确定性情况中的Hurwitz条件保证，因此对应的模型无关PI初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出了一种谱分配方法：利用Lyapunov型算子的谱，通过调整累加因子使原始系统逐渐从稳定的辅助系统逼近，从而获得稳定化控制增益。进一步地，利用系统数据并调整累加因子，设计了一个不依赖初始稳定策略的模型无关算法以实现最优控制。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出的谱分配初始化和模型无关PI算法的有效性，表明该方法能够成功获得初始稳定控制器并实现最优控制。

Conclusion: 本文解决了连续时间不确定随机线性二次控制中模型无关策略迭代的初始化难题，通过谱分配方法获得初始稳定器，并设计了无需初始稳定策略的模型无关算法，为随机系统强化学习控制提供了新的解决途径。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [205] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种新的分布式可诊断性条件，用于在存在传输延迟的情况下验证故障能否在有限步内被协同诊断，并通过延迟记录器和新的诊断函数实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式/分散式故障诊断方法未充分考虑传输延迟对诊断性能的影响，而实际系统中传输延迟普遍存在，可能导致诊断失效。

Method: 提出了一种扩展分散式可诊断性条件的分布式可诊断性条件，并构建了延迟记录器（delay recorder）结构和新的诊断函数来验证该条件。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法能够成功判断故障是否可被诊断。

Conclusion: 该研究为在存在传输延迟的部分可观测离散事件系统中实现分布式故障诊断提供了新的可诊断性判定方法。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [206] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了弱限制增广Zarankiewicz数 z_{wL}(m,3) 在 m=5,6,7,8,9 时的精确值，均为 2m（即 10,12,14,16,18），并通过下界构造和上界精确搜索，强烈支持对所有 m≥5 成立 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 的猜想，同时给出了 BSR(m,3)≥2m 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解弱限制增广Zarankiewicz数在 n=3 情形下的小规模精确值，为验证 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 这一通用猜想提供更多证据，并由此推导BSR的相应下界。

Method: 下界通过显式的弱可允许构造实现；上界（对 m=7,8,9）通过对规范的 C_4-free基图进行精确有限搜索获得。利用 BSR(m,n)≥z_{wL}(m,n) 这一已知关系导出BSR下界。

Result: 得到 z_{wL}(5,3)=10, z_{wL}(6,3)=12, z_{wL}(7,3)=14, z_{wL}(8,3)=16, z_{wL}(9,3)=18，即该范围内 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m；并推出 BSR(m,3)≥2m（m=5,6,7,8,9）。

Conclusion: 这些结果连同已知的小规模情形共同强烈支持 z_{wL}(m,3)=2m 对所有 m≥5 成立的猜想。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [207] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机外梯度（SEG）方法用于单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）的收敛性。研究发现同样本SEG（S-SEG）对样本级Lipschitz参数敏感，仅靠均值Lipschitz性和有界方差无法保证收敛；同时在非有界域上建立了两种SEG变体的高概率受限间隙收敛界，并证明某些改进在一般情况下不可能实现；最后发现一个已知的不对称双步长策略对I-SEG保证几乎必然收敛但对S-SEG会失败，存在使S-SEG几乎必然发散的随机单调VIP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机外梯度（SEG）方法虽然在确定性变分不等式问题上具有成熟的收敛理论，但其随机版本的理解仍不充分。现有分析大多针对独立样本SEG（I-SEG），且需要紧致域或均匀有界方差的强假设；而同样本SEG（S-SEG）因其独特性质很少被研究。本文旨在填补这些理论空白，深化对SEG随机变体收敛行为的理解。

Method: 本文首先构造反例证明S-SEG在仅满足均值Lipschitz性和有界方差时即使在紧致集上也不收敛，说明S-SEG对样本级Lipschitz参数敏感。接着放宽假设集合，对可能非有界域上的两种SEG变体建立高概率受限间隙收敛界，并证明这些结果在一般情况下不能再改进。最后通过构造特定的随机单调VIP，分析非对称双步长策略在S-SEG下的失效性。

Result: （1）S-SEG在均值Lipschitz性和有界方差条件下不保证收敛，即使在紧致域上也是如此；（2）在松弛假设下，I-SEG和S-SEG在非有界域上均获得高概率受限间隙收敛，且该结果在一般情况下是最优的；（3）已知保证I-SEG几乎必然最后迭代收敛的非对称双步长策略对S-SEG失效，存在S-SEG在此步长下几乎必然发散的随机单调VIP。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛性质上的本质差异，证明了S-SEG对样本级Lipschitz参数的高度敏感性，并在更一般条件下建立了两种变体的收敛保证。这些发现不仅填补了随机外梯度方法理论分析的空白，也为实际应用中选择合适的SEG变体提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [208] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从观测的边缘概率密度函数推断广义常微分方程组动力学行为的框架。该方法通过增广状态空间将随机或不可逆过程重铸为确定性可逆流，利用Liouville方程的特征表示进行粒子输运，并通过沿特征线传播的交叉U统计量构建无偏梯度估计器，从而实现随机梯度下降推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中许多随机或不可逆动力系统（如扩散过程）仅能从低维状态空间中观测到边缘概率密度函数，而潜在的随机性和记忆性隐藏在隐变量中。直接从边缘分布推断底层动力学是一个具有挑战性的逆问题，需要新的框架来处理这类不可逆或随机动力学的参数推断。

Method: 将低维状态下的扩散及其他不可逆过程通过增广状态空间重铸为确定性可逆流，使联合密度满足双曲型Liouville方程；观测的边缘分布是该双曲动力学在观测坐标上的投影。算法利用Liouville方程的特征表示而非直接求解高维方程，将初始分布采样的粒子沿特征线输运；欧拉敏感性通过沿特征线传播获得，结合交叉U统计量产生无偏梯度估计器，实现随机梯度下降。

Result: 通过四个实验验证了方法有效性：三模态线性系统的单模态边缘观测恢复、带隐模态的非线性Gompertz增长模型、隐模态将单峰边缘变为双峰的双稳态系统，以及细胞流中粒子的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复。同时分析了各设置下的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将随机/不可逆动力学推断转化为确定性ODE从边缘观测中的恢复问题，巧妙地规避了高维Liouville方程求解的困难，为不可逆或随机动力学系统的参数推断提供了新途径。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [209] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的应用，发现该更新存在精确的闭式解，并据此提出了名为Skewon的高效算法，同时给出了光滑非凸情形下的一阶收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stiefel流形在机器学习和科学计算中广泛存在，现有的Muon方法在该流形上的扩展依赖启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率不一。作者希望找到精确且高效的更新方式。

Method: 作者证明了Stiefel流形上的Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，基于此结果开发了Skewon算法，并提供了高效的实现方式。

Result: Skewon算法在光滑非凸设定下具有一阶收敛性保证，且实现效率优于现有的启发式或迭代方法。

Conclusion: 通过揭示Muon在Stiefel流形上的闭式解结构，Skewon为正交约束优化提供了一种兼具理论保证与实用效率的新方法。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [210] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有界区域上多维连续性方程（含源项）的局部内部通量反馈镇定问题。反馈通过通量散度施加，使误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程。在有限时间特征阻尼不等式的几何条件下，结合增益条件与Lyapunov型入口条件，建立了误差在L2中的指数稳定性，并给出了输入到状态（ISS）估计、椭圆右逆反馈实现及非线性速度场扩展，最终以二维算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维连续性方程在有界域上的镇定是分布式参数控制中的核心难题，现有方法通常需要全局观测或强几何假设。本文旨在通过仅依赖通量散度的局部内部反馈实现对参考剖面的误差镇定，并克服压缩放大效应与局部阻尼之间的耦合困难，从而为更一般的多维输运/连续方程系统提供可验证的稳定性判据。

Method: 核心方法是构造有限的"特征阻尼不等式"作为几何条件——要求相关特征线在有限时间内积累均匀的阻尼量。在该条件下，结合增益条件（局部阻尼需超越压缩放大带来的指数增长），利用加权Lyapunov泛函推导微分Lyapunov判据，进而得到L2指数稳定性。此外，通过Lyapunov型入口条件约束轨线族以验证特征阻尼；利用椭圆右逆实现反馈通量；并将特征阻尼论证推广至速度场非线性依赖于状态的情形。

Result: 本文证明了在有限时间特征阻尼不等式与适当的增益条件下，误差在L2范数意义下指数稳定；建立了加权Lyapunov泛函下的微分判据，并导出了关于加性扰动的输入到状态（ISS）估计。同时给出了反馈通量的椭圆右逆实现方案，并将结果推广到非线性速度场。二维算例验证了几何条件、增益条件和实现条件的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多维连续性方程通过局部内部通量反馈镇定的完整理论框架，揭示了特征阻尼、压缩放大与反馈增益三者之间的定量关系，为带源项连续性方程在有界域的实用镇定提供了可验证的设计准则与稳定性保证。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [211] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散事件系统中观测信息可能延迟到达场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了延迟观测下诊断与预测的新条件，并指出现有"可预测性"概念需修正以避免保守性，最后通过延迟观测器和新的验证函数来验证这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际离散事件系统中，传感器观测数据可能因通信延迟等原因滞后到达，现有故障诊断与预测方法通常假设观测即时可用，这导致在延迟观测场景下已有方法可能过于保守或失效，需要重新审视和修正相关概念。

Method: 文章首先提出了延迟观测下故障诊断与预测的两个条件（诊断性和可预测性条件），通过分析指出现有可预测性定义需修正以消除保守性；随后引入了延迟观测器（delay observer）来构造考虑延迟的系统观测模型，并设计了一种新的验证函数（verification function）来系统性地检验所提出的诊断性和可预测性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法能够有效验证延迟观测场景下的故障诊断与预测条件，证明了修正后的可预测性概念及延迟观测器+验证函数框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该项工作为存在观测延迟的离散事件系统提供了一套完整的故障诊断与预测理论框架，通过修正可预测性概念克服了传统方法的保守性，所提出的验证机制在理论上被证明行之有效。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [212] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在电力系统规划中采用集成方法，将稳定措施（惯性响应和动态电压稳定性约束）与发电、输电和储能的扩展同步考虑，发现相比传统顺序规划方法可降低系统成本，且构网型电池储能系统的双重用途优于STATCOM等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器型资源（IBR）在电力系统中的渗透率不断提高，同步发电机对动态电压稳定性和惯性响应的贡献需要被替代。然而，现有的电力系统规划方法是顺序式的，稳定措施仅在规划过程的后期才被考虑，这可能导致次优的规划结果。

Method: 提出一种集成式电力系统规划方法，将稳定措施与发电、输电和储能系统的扩展同步考虑，通过简化的惯性约束和电压稳定性约束来实现，并与传统的顺序规划方法进行对比分析。

Result: 集成方法能够降低系统成本；构网型电池储能系统的双重用途（同时提供惯性和电压支撑）比其他稳定措施（如STATCOM）更具优势。

Conclusion: 研究表明，采用集成的电力系统规划方法比顺序式方法更经济高效，其中构网型电池储能系统作为双重用途的稳定措施是最优选择。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>
